# GOAL: GAMMA-STATION-2026-09-13

> J verbatim (2026-09-13 ~13:40 ET, Sunday, `/goal`): *"Please set up my autonomous gamma once and for all … I want it here … move literally
> everything over to E … Get Ollama over there. Get project 42 copied over there … I have Wyze webcams … why do I need a Raspberry Pi? Why can't I
> just plug HDMI into my TV … You should be able to set this up in the next five days. I can drop down to the hundred dollar a month plan after 9/15,
> and we'll use the rest of our two hundred dollar month plan to get this all set up … If you don't [have enough data], ask me. But if you do, then
> that's your goal to set it up end to end."*
> Amendment 13:5x ET: *"weigh the options of hard disk vs M.2 speeds and how the LLM runs on them before you go moving things."* Weighed: all three
> drives are SSDs; E: is the only NVMe (970 EVO, ~3.4 GB/s vs ~0.5 GB/s SATA on C:/D:); disk speed affects model load/swap time only, never tok/s → E:.

Parent plan: [`markdown/planning/GAMMA-STATION.md`](../../../markdown/planning/GAMMA-STATION.md) (§3c Option A: brain on this PC, no new box).
Hard deadline: **2026-09-18** (five days; Max 20x → Max 5x after 09-15). Model tier: Fable does ops steps + specs; every code build is a Sonnet spawn (≤2/day).
Absorbs GOAL-EARN-YOUR-KEEP-2026-09-12 item (5) (the 09-18 honest-state verdict) — that goal is re-queued directly behind this one on the ladder; its
in-flight items (1) and (7) keep their builders and their own PROGRESS LOG.

## DONE-WHEN (falsifiable; each checked by a command or ledger row quoted in the PROGRESS LOG)
- (a) **The Station runs unattended for 24 h on this PC with a local brain and shows on the TV.** Check: `automation/state/station/loop-ledger.jsonl` has ≥48 rows
  over a 24 h span with `model` = the local planner and `status` ∈ {ok, yielded}; a screenshot of the TV display (or the kiosk window) taken by
  `station_kiosk.ps1 -Snapshot` carries a HOME timestamp < 35 min old.
- (b) **Gamma brought ideas nobody prompted.** Check: `automation/state/station/ideas-board.json` holds ≥3 cards with `evidence`, `proposed_shadow_test`,
  `cost_line` and `prompted_by: "station-loop"` written on ≥2 different days.
- (c) **Automation no longer bills the Max pool.** Check: one full day (a blackout day) with every `claude` fire routed to local or parked, quoted from
  `SCHEDULED-TASKS.md` + the per-fire launch env; `core-decisions.jsonl` shows no RTH gap > 3 min that day.
- (d) **An off-box alarm exists.** Check: a deliberate 15-minute silence of the heartbeat ping produces an alert on J's phone (alert-only; kill-type action waits
  for the 09-29 checkpoint).
- (e) **The trading path is byte-identical.** Check: `git diff 606e8640..HEAD --stat -- setup/scripts/heartbeat_core.py automation/state/fleet/ backtest/lib/filters.py backtest/lib/risk_gate.py automation/prompts/heartbeat*.md params*.json` is empty (freeze to 10-30 honoured).
- A null is a valid terminal state: if the local planner cannot pass the Claude-Code smoke test at ≥16K context after (3), the goal records that and falls back
  to the qwen3.6:35b floor for the loop — still counted against (a)/(b), reported plainly in HONEST STATE.

## OPERATING RULES
- **Freeze (→ 10-30):** nothing here edits `heartbeat_core`, fleet, filters, risk_gate, params, heartbeat prompts, or Task Scheduler entries on the trading path.
  The live engine keeps running from `C:\Users\jackw\Desktop\42`; the E: clone is the Station's working copy. Moving the live checkout to E: is a Phase-4 item
  (directory junction, after 10-30), not this goal.
- **No LLM on the live tick; paper only; no live keys on the Station side.** The loop reads ledgers and writes `automation/state/station/*` and docs — never orders.
- **Silent rig:** every new process is a hidden chain or a service; no console windows (J 09-05). The loop **yields** when a fullscreen game or GPU load > 50 % is
  detected, and never runs heavy work 09:30–15:55 ET.
- **Routing:** local models reach Claude Code through per-process env (`ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL` → Ollama, the `launch_claude_local.ps1` pattern). Never a router
  under J's interactive tools (scars 07-14, 08-23). J's desktop app stays on Anthropic direct.
- **Model promotion:** every model enters a role through `shadow_model_eval` (≥85 % over ≥15 days). The planner ships as `gamma-planner` on a scorecard clock;
  until it passes, its outputs are labelled UNVERIFIED on the board.
- **Token pacing:** Fable = ops steps + specs; ≤2 sequential Sonnet builders/day (`model:"sonnet"` explicit), each with objective / return schema / scope / do-not list.
  Per-day token estimate in the PROGRESS LOG. Use the remaining Max 20x through 09-15 (J), then Max 5x.
- Every fire: `python setup/scripts/conductor_outcome.py record --task-id GOAL-GAMMA-STATION-2026-09-13 --drained <n> --added <n> --lessons <n> --tests-delta <n> --regressions <n> --note "<note>"`.
- `STATUS.md` gets a line at OPEN and CLOSE only. CLAIM an item (`[ ]` → `[~]`) before working it. Never `/loop` this goal in-session.
- Secrets J only: Wyze credentials, healthchecks URL, any key — pasted by J into gitignored homes; Fable never types them.

- [2026-09-15 21:22:26 Tuesday EDT] PROBE-FAILS 0d2057ab (deploy sLVHdAoagioLfbyvqjSd7): fixed the two FAILs from probe 20260916T005621Z (build AIUCqlpwtxiI7kYDCb1aJ). **walker_separation** (min pairwise 0.19u, worst pair a974762c.../a154676b...): differential against the samples showed both agents "spawning" at the SAME sample tick (t=45.9s) and flipping to "walking" at the SAME next tick (t=47.4s), then walking the shared gate-exit corridor in lockstep to two DIFFERENT final targets (ambient-core vs hub-center) -- confirmed `findCarAhead`'s own `destKey` pre-filter never engages between them (by design, not a gap) and `routeViaGraph` (d3da0581) is not involved (grep confirms only Agent.tsx's resident-walk path calls it, never LiveAgents.tsx). Root cause: `computeBatchOrder(newIds)` in LiveAgents.tsx's reconcile effect restarts its own batch index at 0 on EVERY poll -- two ids that are each the FIRST new id in their OWN poll (different reconcile polls a few hundred ms apart, well within "spawned within the same second") both get batch index 0, hence the identical 0s stagger delay, despite spawning close enough in wall time to still be on the same shared corridor leg. Fix: `liveAgentWalk.ts#advanceCoalescedSpawnBatch` (new, pure, CONVOY-STACK v15) -- a reducer LiveAgents.tsx threads through two new refs (`spawnBatchStateRef`/`leaveBatchStateRef`) that carries the running batch index FORWARD across polls landing within `SPAWN_BATCH_COALESCE_MS` (2000ms) of the previous spawn, resetting to 0 after a real gap so an ordinary lone spawn late in a session never inherits an ever-growing delay. `computeBatchOrder`/`computeBatchStaggerDelays` themselves are UNCHANGED (existing tests untouched); this is an additional layer applied at both call sites (spawn batch + leave batch, same mechanism, same fix). 4 new tests in `tests/live-agent-walk.test.ts` pin: cross-poll near-simultaneous spawns get distinct indices (the exact probe shape), a post-window spawn resets to 0, same-poll ordering matches `computeBatchOrder`'s tie-break, and an empty poll never perturbs the running state. **label_vs_screen_overlap** (twin-monitor-33/34 overlapFrac 0.25/0.29): traced commit 77e8308f's actual diff -- it shipped the AUDIT check (`hq-scene-audit.ts#checkLabelScreenOverlap`) but its `labelDeclutter.ts`/`LabelDeclutterManager.tsx` changes were the UNRELATED legibility-floor fix in the same pass; no runtime keep-out was ever wired for the two TwinMonitors screens. `LabelDeclutterManager.tsx`'s `resolveLabelOffsets` obstacles arg only ever received `[data-hq-obstacle]` DOM HUD rects (help bar, panels) -- never the 3D screen meshes, so a label could sit directly on either screen with zero nudge, at ANY priority (the resolver's own pre-existing "obstacle always outranks every label" rule was never the problem -- it was just never given the screen rects). Fix: new `labelDeclutter.ts#ndcCornersToViewportRect` (pure, mirrors `hq-scene-audit.ts#projectAabbToViewportRect`'s own math so the runtime keep-out and the offline probe check agree) + `LabelDeclutterManager.tsx#readScreenObstacleRects` (traverses `store.scene` every tick for the SAME `userData.hqKind==="screen"`/`hqInformational` tags TwinMonitors.tsx already carries -- no change to TwinMonitors.tsx needed or made -- projects each screen's live world AABB via the real camera, appends to the existing obstacles array). 3 new tests in `tests/hq-label-declutter.test.ts`: NDC->pixel projection math, null-on-bad-input, and a label centred on a keep-out rect (both GAMMA and LIVE_AGENT priority tiers) resolving fully outside it or fading. Verified: `npm test` 665/665 pass (up from 658); `npx tsc --noEmit -p .` clean on all 6 touched files (only pre-existing TS5097 test-import noise in untouched files, unchanged); `--rescore` of the original FAIL samples reproduces the identical walker_separation FAIL (0.1881u, same pair) confirming the CHECK code itself is untouched, not loosened; `dashboard_deploy.ps1 -Tag PROBE-FAILS` -> "DEPLOY OK build_id=sLVHdAoagioLfbyvqjSd7 elapsed_s=66"; fresh `hq_live_probe.py --plausibility` against that build -- 7/7 PASS (wall_penetration, screen_facing, desk_clearance, desk_orientation, walker_wall_cross, label_legibility, **label_vs_screen_overlap**). - [2026-09-15 21:49:36 Tuesday EDT] KEEPOUT-MOVING-CAM 0feb9bed: fixed label_vs_screen_overlap FAIL from probe 20260916T012329Z (twin-monitor-101/102, overlapFrac 0.19/0.30, worst labels "Coach"/"Claude (you)"). TWO real, independently-provable mechanisms, not the runtime declutter resolver's collision/priority logic itself (already correct): (1) STALE AUDIT SNAPSHOT -- hq_live_probe.py's `compute_plausibility_verdicts` called `window.__hqSceneAudit()` (screen viewport rects) exactly ONCE before its own ~4x/s label-sampling loop began, per that function's own doc comment ("static scene geometry doesn't change tick-to-tick, so one snapshot is sufficient") -- true of world geometry, false of a screen's PROJECTED viewport rect under the real full-probe's auto-orbiting camera (no `?tour=0`), so a label sampled tens of ticks later was compared against a screen position from an earlier, different camera pose. (2) UNDER-FADE ON CAPPED NUDGE -- proven by unit math, not guesswork: `resolveLabelOffsets`'s existing cap-and-fade path drops an uncleared label to the generic `DEFAULT_FADE_OPACITY` (0.35), but hq_probe_lib.py's own check only excludes opacity < 0.05 -- so a label pinned against a screen mesh bigger than the nudge budget (a real monitor's projected AABB can exceed the default ~60px cap on both axes) stayed a counted violation even after "doing its best". Fix: (a) `hq-scene-audit.ts#computeScreenViewportRectsReport` (new, lightweight, screen-only traversal) + `window.__hqSceneAuditScreens` hook, called by hq_live_probe.py on EVERY plausibility tick and paired with that same tick's label rects (`screen_rects` field, per-sample); `hq_probe_lib.py#check_label_screen_overlap` now prefers a sample's own per-tick screens over the old single global list, falling back for older/failed-eval samples. (b) `ObstacleRect.isScreen` (new flag, set only by `LabelDeclutterManager.tsx#readScreenObstacleRects`) makes `resolveLabelOffsets` fade a still-colliding label FULLY to opacity 0 when the obstacle it failed to clear is a screen -- generic HUD-obstacle fade behavior (0.35) is unchanged (pinned by a new test). Tests: `dashboard/tests/hq-label-declutter.test.ts` +3 (real-probe-shaped screen-too-big-for-cap fades to 0; non-screen obstacle keeps fadeOpacity; tightened the pre-existing centred-on-screen test to require full clear or opacity 0, no more accepting a bare 0.35 as a pass) -- `npm test` 667/667 pass (up from 665, 36/36 in the touched file); `setup/scripts/test_hq_probe_lib.py` +3 (per-tick screens preferred over stale global, fallback to global when a sample lacks per-tick data, NO-DATA when neither exists) -- 119/119 pass. `npx tsc --noEmit -p .` clean (only pre-existing TS5097 import-extension noise in untouched test files). Deploy: `DEPLOY OK build_id=W1X8-sZC8g0MWi-sC6BtG elapsed_s=60`. Verify: could NOT pin the exact failing camera pose from the FAIL run -- `compute_plausibility_verdicts` never persisted per-tick label/camera/screen data to the samples file (only the final aggregated verdict), so `--rescore` cannot replay it and this pass could not extract one; ran `hq_live_probe.py --plausibility --plausibility-seconds 60` instead against the SAME real condition that caused the original FAIL (default URL, no `?tour=0`/`?cam=` override -- camera auto-orbiting the whole run) -- fresh run: scene_ready=true, hardware GL, 3825 label samples, screenCount 25/readableScreenCount 2, **label_vs_screen_overlap: PASS, 0 violations** (was FAIL 2 violations); label_legibility also still PASS (239 faded-excluded, no regression from the new fade-to-0 path). UNVERIFIED: the exact original failing tick/pose (raw per-tick data was never recorded pre-fix, which is itself part of what (1) above now fixes going forward -- a future FAIL will carry real per-tick screen_rects for a true `--rescore` repro).
